```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import { Panel } from 'components/atoms/Panel';
import { CollapsiblePanel } from 'components/atoms/CollapsiblePanel';
import { ContainerWithIcon, Position } from 'components/atoms/ContainerWithIcon';
import { IAccordionProps } from './types';
/** 
* Wrapper component that conditionally renders `Panel` or `CollapsedPanel` and passes children. 
*/
/** 
* Wrapper component that conditionally renders `Panel` or `CollapsedPanel` and passes children. 
*/
const Accordion = (props: IAccordionProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { items } = props;
  return (    
<>      
{items.map(({ expandComponent, collapsedComponent, isCollapsable, onInfoIconClick, ariaLabel, isOpened, id }) => {        
let accessibilityProps: any = {          
expanded: 'true',        
};        
if (id) {          
accessibilityProps = {            
...accessibilityProps,            
id,          
};        
}        
// cloning prop in order to pass 'expanded' prop to the CoverageProductListColapsedItem component        
const clonedCollapsedComponent: JSX.Element = React.cloneElement(collapsedComponent, accessibilityProps);
        const expandedChildren = (          
<>            
{expandComponent}            
<ContainerWithIcon              
name={ICON_TYPES.INFORMATION}              
action={onInfoIconClick}              
position={Position.BOTTOM_RIGHT}              
ariaLabel='More information'>              
{clonedCollapsedComponent}            
</ContainerWithIcon>          
</>        
);        
return (          
<React.Fragment key={`accordion_item-${id}`}>            
{isCollapsable ? (              
<CollapsiblePanel                
nestLevel={1}                
collapsedChildren={collapsedComponent}                
expandedChildren={expandedChildren}                
initiallyCollapsed={!isOpened}                
ariaLabel={ariaLabel}              
/>            
) : (              
<Panel nestLevel={1}>{expandedChildren}</Panel>            
)}          
</React.Fragment>        
);      
})}    
</>  
)}          
</React.Fragment>        
);      
})}    
</>  
);
};
export { Accordion };
```

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { SCAccordionArrowWithText } from './styles';
import { IAccordionArrowWithTextProps } from './types';
import { ReactComponent as ArrowDarkDown } from './svg/arrowDarkDown.svg';
import { ReactComponent as ArrowDarkUp } from './svg/arrowDarkUp.svg';
import { ReactComponent as ArrowLightDown } from './svg/arrowLightDown.svg';
import { ReactComponent as ArrowLightUp } from './svg/arrowLightUp.svg';
/** 
* AccordionArrowWithText 
* @summary 
* Arrow svg icon with text on the left, used in SimpleAccordionGroup and AOX SelectedProductAccountAccordion 
* @param expanded 
* Expanded state if expanded = true, down vs up arrow 
* @param displayMode 
* @param expandedText 
* Optional expanded state text to override Hide Details default 
* @param collapsedText 
* @param collapsedText 
* Optional collapsed state text to override View Details default 
*/
const AccordionArrowWithText = ({  
expanded,  
displayMode,  
expandedText,  
collapsedText,  
className,
}: IAccordionArrowWithTextProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();  
const ArrowUp = displayMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ArrowLightUp : ArrowDarkUp;  
const ArrowDown = displayMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ArrowLightDown : ArrowDarkDown;
  return (    
<SCAccordionArrowWithText expanded={expanded} displayMode={displayMode} className={className}>      
{expanded ? expandedText || t('GLOBAL__HIDE_DETAILS') : collapsedText || t('GLOBAL__VIEW_DETAILS')}      
{expanded ? <ArrowUp /> : <ArrowDown />}    
</SCAccordionArrowWithText>  
);
};
export { AccordionArrowWithText };
```

);
};
export { AccordionArrowWithText };

```typescript
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { IFormInputOption } from 'core';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles';
import { SCAddCustomOptionsWrapper, SCCoreButton, SCAddCustomOptionWrapper } from './styles';
import { IAddCustomOptionsProps } from './types';
const AddCustomOptions = ({  
name = '',  
onChange,  
className,  
label,  
id,  
disabled,  
value,  
...props
}: IAddCustomOptionsProps): JSX.Element => {  
const [customOption, setCustomOption] = useState<IFormInputOption>({ label: '', value: '', payload: 'custom' });  
const customOptionStringValue = customOption.value.toString();  
const customAddButtonLabel = label ? `+ Add ${label}`.toLocaleUpperCase() : '';  
const handleAddCustomOption = () => {    
const parsedValue = value ? (JSON.parse(value) as IFormInputOption[]) : [];    
const hasDuplicateValue = parsedValue.map(({ value }) => value).includes(customOptionStringValue);    
if (!customOptionStringValue || hasDuplicateValue) return;    
const formattedForParsingFromFormValueOptions = JSON.stringify([...parsedValue, customOption]);    
const formattedForParsingFromFormValueOptions = JSON.stringify([...parsedValue, customOption]);    
onChange?.(name, formattedForParsingFromFormValueOptions);    
setCustomOption({ label: '', value: '', payload: 'custom' });  
};
  return (    
<SCAddCustomOptionsWrapper className={className} id={`${id}-custom-checkbox-wrapper`}>      
<SCAddCustomOptionWrapper>        
<SCUtilityInput          
{...props}          
onChange={(event) => {            
const {              
target: { value },            
} = event;            
setCustomOption({              
label: value,              
value,              
payload: 'custom',            
});          
}}          
disabled={disabled}          
value={customOptionStringValue}          
id={`${id}-custom-checkbox-input-field`}        
/>        
<SCCoreButton          
disabled={disabled}          
isSecondary          
onClick={() => handleAddCustomOption()}          
id={`${id}-custom-checkbox-add-button`}>          
{customAddButtonLabel}        
</SCCoreButton>      
</SCAddCustomOptionWrapper>    
</SCAddCustomOptionsWrapper>  
);
};
export { AddCustomOptions };
```

import { IAnchorLink } from './types';
import { SCAnchorLink } from './styles';
/** 
* Renders a react scrollchor link element. Element attributes will be passed through 
* @param {string} text - required text for link 
* @param {string} className? - optional className for needed css overrides 
* @param {string} id? - optional id for style overrides or QA testing 
* @param {string} to - required page id needed for anchor 
* @param {function} beforeAnimate? - function executed before animation 
* @param {function} afterAnimate? - function executed after animation 
* @param {object} animate? - object defining animate properties 
* @param {boolean} disableHistory? - boolean to decide whether link is shown in url 
* @param {boolean} show? - boolean to decide whether link is visible 
* @param {number} offset? - positive or negative number setting the offset from the anchor for final positioning 
* @param {number} duration? - duration of animation in ms 
* @param {boolean} active? - boolean to decide whether link is active 
*/
export const AnchorLink = ({  
text,  
className = '',  
id = '',  
to,  
beforeAnimate = () => {},  
afterAnimate = () => {},  
afterAnimate = () => {},  
animate,  
disableHistory,  
show = true,  
active = false,
}: IAnchorLink) => (  
<SCAnchorLink    
id={id}    
to={`${to}`}    
className={`${className} ${show ? '' : 'hide'} ${active ? 'active' : ''}`}    
beforeAnimate={beforeAnimate}    
afterAnimate={afterAnimate}    
animate={animate}    
disableHistory={disableHistory}>    
{text}  
</SCAnchorLink>
);
```

import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { joinNonEmpty, prefixIfValue, formatDateMMDDYYYY } from 'helpers/formatters';
import { LoanTypes, LoanTypeImages } from './constants';
import {  
SCMainContainer,  
SCContent,  
SCDataValues,  
SCDate,  
SCHeader,  
SCIcon,  
SCName,  
SCStatus,  
SCTitle,  
SCSeparator,
} from './styles';
import { ApplicationViewModel, ApplicationType } from './types';
/** 
* @param application - all of the data required to display, as well as a type describing which fields are expected to be filled 
* @returns Component list item used in the MSRGlobalSearchResults for Applications 
*/
export const ApplicationListItem = (application: ApplicationViewModel): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();  
const {    
applicationType,    
applicationType,    
appNumber,    
title,    
appStatus,    
dateSubmitted,    
numProducts,    
primaryApplicant,    
secondaryApplicant,    
loanOfficerFirstName,    
loanOfficerLastName,    
losLoanNumber,    
loanStatus,    
loanAmount,    
type,  
} = application;
  const primaryType: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', primaryApplicant.type);  
const primaryId: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', primaryApplicant.id);  
const primaryFullName: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', primaryApplicant.lastName, primaryApplicant.firstName);  
const primaryDetails: string = joinNonEmpty(    
', ',    
prefixIfValue(`${t('ID')} `, primaryApplicant.id),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('SSN')} -`, primaryApplicant.ssnLast4)  
);
  const secondaryType: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', secondaryApplicant?.type);  
prefixIfValue(`${t('ID')} `, primaryApplicant.id),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('SSN')} -`, primaryApplicant.ssnLast4)  
);
  const secondaryType: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', secondaryApplicant?.type);  
const secondaryFullName: string = joinNonEmpty(', ', secondaryApplicant?.lastName, secondaryApplicant?.firstName);  
const secondaryDetails: string = joinNonEmpty(    
', ',    
prefixIfValue(`${t('ID')} `, secondaryApplicant?.id),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('SSN')} -`, secondaryApplicant?.ssnLast4)  
);
  const aoxAppDetails: string[] = [    
prefixIfValue(`${primaryType}: `, joinNonEmpty(' ', primaryFullName, primaryDetails)),    
prefixIfValue(`${secondaryType}: `, joinNonEmpty(' ', secondaryFullName, secondaryDetails)),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('PRODUCTS_LABEL')}: `, numProducts),  
].filter((x) => x !== '' && x !== undefined);
  const loanOfficerFullName = joinNonEmpty(' ', loanOfficerFirstName, loanOfficerLastName);  
const dlxLoanNumberAndTitle = joinNonEmpty(' ', losLoanNumber, title);  
const loanAppDetails: string[] = [    
prefixIfValue(`${t('PRIMARY_LABEL')}: `, joinNonEmpty(' ', primaryFullName, primaryDetails)),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('JOINT_LABEL')}: `, joinNonEmpty(' ', secondaryFullName, secondaryDetails)),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('LOAN_AMOUNT_LABEL')}: `, loanAmount ? `$${loanAmount}` : ''),    
prefixIfValue(`${t('LOAN_OFFICER_LABEL')}: `, loanOfficerFullName),  
].filter((x) => x !== '' && x !== undefined);
  const loanTypeImgMap: Record<string, string> = {    
].filter((x) => x !== '' && x !== undefined);
  const loanTypeImgMap: Record<string, string> = {    
[LoanTypes.auto]: LoanTypeImages.auto,    
[LoanTypes['credit card']]: LoanTypeImages['credit card'],    
[LoanTypes.recreational]: LoanTypeImages.recreational,    
[LoanTypes.account]: LoanTypeImages.personal,    
[LoanTypes.personal]: LoanTypeImages.personal,    
[LoanTypes['home equity']]: LoanTypeImages['home equity'],    
[LoanTypes.secured]: LoanTypeImages.secured,    
'': '',  
};
  if (applicationType === ApplicationType.DlxGlobalResult) {    
return (      
<SCMainContainer tabIndex={0} id={`loanAppListItem_${primaryId}`}>        
<SCIcon alt={t('LOAN_TYPE_ALT')} src={loanTypeImgMap[type?.toLowerCase() ?? '']} />        
<SCContent>          
<SCHeader>            
<SCTitle>              
<SCName>{`${dlxLoanNumberAndTitle}`}</SCName>              
<SCSeparator>|</SCSeparator> <wbr />              
<SCStatus>{loanStatus}</SCStatus>            
</SCTitle>            
<SCDate>{formatDateMMDDYYYY(dateSubmitted)}</SCDate>          
</SCHeader>          
</SCHeader>          
<SCDataValues>            
{loanAppDetails.map((detail, i) => {              
return (                
<>                  
{i !== loanAppDetails.length - 1 ? `${detail}, ` : detail}                  
<wbr />                
</>              
);            
})}          
</SCDataValues>        
</SCContent>      
</SCMainContainer>    
);  
}
  return (    
<SCMainContainer tabIndex={0} id={`appListItem_${appNumber}`}>      
<SCIcon alt={t('APP_ALT')} src={LoanTypeImages.secured} />      
<SCContent>        
<SCHeader>          
<SCTitle>            
<SCName>{`${appNumber} ${title}`}</SCName>            
<SCSeparator>|</SCSeparator> <wbr />            
<SCStatus>{appStatus}</SCStatus>          
</SCTitle>          
<SCDate>{formatDateMMDDYYYY(dateSubmitted)}</SCDate>        
</SCHeader>        
<SCDataValues>          
{aoxAppDetails.map((detail, i) => {            
return (              
<>                
{i !== aoxAppDetails.length - 1 ? `${detail}, ` : detail}                
<wbr />              
</>            
);          
})}        
</SCDataValues>      
</SCContent>    
</SCMainContainer>  
);
};
```

import React from 'react';
import './avatar.css';
// Use the following to implement small or medium avatar sizes:
//    <Avatar fName={'Andrea'} lName={'Cronin'} size={'md'}/>
// If size parameter is left off the default is large
// Available sizes are sm: 24X24, md: 48x48; lg: 56X56
export const Avatar = ({ size, fName, lName, idName }: any): JSX.Element => {  
const idNameSuffix = idName ? `_${idName}` : '';
// Available sizes are sm: 24X24, md: 48x48; lg: 56X56
export const Avatar = ({ size, fName, lName, idName }: any): JSX.Element => {  
const idNameSuffix = idName ? `_${idName}` : '';
  return (    
<div      
id={`avatarCircle${idNameSuffix}`}      
aria-label={`Avatar${idNameSuffix}`}      
className='avatarCircle'      
data-testid='avatarCircle'>      
<div        
id={`AvatarInitials${idNameSuffix}`}        
aria-label='Avatar Initials'        
className={size === 'sm' ? 'small' : size === 'md' ? 'medium' : 'large'}>        
{fName.charAt(0) + lName.charAt(0)}      
</div>    
</div>  
);
};
```

import React from 'react';
import { SCBasicInput, SCBasicInputError } from './styles';
import { IBasicInputProps } from './types';
/** 
* Base level input component. Primarily used by the `Input` component. 
*/
const BasicInput = (props: IBasicInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const BasicInput = (props: IBasicInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { onChange, onBlur, onFocus, label, invalid, value, error, errorId, maxLength, ...rest } = props;
  return (    
<>      
<SCBasicInput        
onChange={onChange}        
onBlur={onBlur}        
onFocus={onFocus}        
aria-label={label}        
invalid={invalid}        
value={value}        
maxLength={maxLength}        
{...rest}      
/>      
{invalid && error && <SCBasicInputError id={errorId}>{error}</SCBasicInputError>}    
</>  
);
};
export { BasicInput };

import React from 'react';
import { ReactComponent as Ellipse } from './svg/Ellipse8.svg';
import { SCBulletPointText } from './styles';
import { IBulletPointTextProps } from './types';
/** 
* BulletPointText 
* @summary 
* Bullet point with custom "ellipse" SVG taken from Figma 
* Should be rendered inside a <ul></ul> for accessibility purposes 
* Bullet SVG, font style, and spacing from bullet matches commonly used bullet points 
* across different components on Figma. Supports LIGHT and DARK modes 
* Bullet point should be vertically aligned with center of FIRST LINE of text 
* @param {React.ReactNode} children 
* Children should usually be string, but keeping it as generic ReactNode in case we want markdown, images, etc. 
* @param {DisplayMode} displayMode 
*/
const BulletPointText = ({ children, className, displayMode }: IBulletPointTextProps): JSX.Element => {  
return (    
<SCBulletPointText className={className} displayMode={displayMode}>      
<Ellipse aria-hidden />      
<span>{children}</span>    
const BulletPointText = ({ children, className, displayMode }: IBulletPointTextProps): JSX.Element => {  
return (    
<SCBulletPointText className={className} displayMode={displayMode}>      
<Ellipse aria-hidden />      
<span>{children}</span>    
</SCBulletPointText>  
);
};
export { BulletPointText };

import { Theme, DisplayMode } from 'styles';
import styled, { css, CSSProp } from 'styled-components';
export const SCBulletPointText = styled.li<{ displayMode?: DisplayMode }>`  
display: flex;  
flex-direction: row;
  ${Theme.getFontStyles('body2')};  
color: ${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
css`      
${Theme.getColor(displayMode === DisplayMode.DARK ? 'contrastGreyscale02' : 'contrastGreyscale08')}    
`};  
svg {    
/** Margin top to keep bullet point vertically centered       
* with first line of text  */    
margin-top: 7px;    
/** Min height and width to stop bullet point from       
* being compressed by margin  */    
min-height: 6px;

...props
}: IProps): JSX.Element => (

id={id}    
className={className}    
onClick={onClick}    
type='button'    
disabled={disabled}    
displayMode={displayMode}    
tabIndex={tabIndex}    
{...props}>    
{children}  
</SCPrimaryButton>
);

```typescript
import React, { useCallback, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Panel, IPanelProps } from '../Panel';
import { ICON_TYPES } from '../Icon';
import { ContainerWithIcon, Position } from '../ContainerWithIcon';
import { SCDiv } from './styles';
import { ICollapsiblePanelProps } from './types';
/** 
* Wrapper for the `Panel` component. 
* Allows panel to collapse by passing `expandedChildren` or `collapsedChildren`. 
*/
const CollapsiblePanel = ({  
collapsedChildren,  
expandedChildren,  
initiallyCollapsed,  
ariaLabel,  
...props
}: ICollapsiblePanelProps & Omit<IPanelProps, 'children'>): JSX.Element => {  
const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(initiallyCollapsed);  
useEffect(() => {    
setCollapsed(initiallyCollapsed);  
}, [initiallyCollapsed]);  
const toggleCollapse = useCallback(() => {    
setCollapsed(!collapsed);  
}, [collapsed, setCollapsed]);  
const iconType = collapsed ? ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_DOWN : ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_UP;  
const position = collapsed ? Position.MIDDLE_RIGHT : Position.TOP_RIGHT;  
return (    
<section>      
<Panel {...props}>        
<ContainerWithIcon          
name={iconType}          
action={toggleCollapse}          
position={position}          
ariaExpanded={!collapsed}          
ariaLabel={ariaLabel}>          
<SCDiv collapsed={collapsed}>{collapsed ? collapsedChildren : expandedChildren}</SCDiv>        
</ContainerWithIcon>      
</Panel>    
</section>  
);
};
CollapsiblePanel.defaultProps = {  
initiallyCollapsed: true,
};
export { CollapsiblePanel };

import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useCombobox, UseComboboxState } from 'downshift';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import { CoreButton } from 'components/atoms/CoreButton';
import { IOption } from 'core/entities/loanApplication';
import { escapeNewRegExpString } from 'helpers/utils';
import { shouldFilterOnKeyUp } from 'types/generics';
import {  
SCCombobox,  
SCComboboxInput,  
SCComboboxLabel,  
SCComboboxLabelIcon,  
SCComboboxSubtext,  
SCInputErrorMessage,  
SCInputWrap,  
SCMenu,  
SCMenuItem,  
SCMenuToggle,  
SCMenuWrap,  
SCNoOptionsMessage,
} from './styles';
import { IComboboxProps } from './types';
import { FixedContainer } from '../FixedContainer';
const UP_ARROW_KEY = 'ArrowUp';
const DOWN_ARROW_KEY = 'ArrowDown';
/** 
* Combobox component used across loan-application, DynamicForm, and UI. 
*/
const Combobox = ({  
id,  
name,  
value = '',  
options,  
onChange,  
onBlur,  
placeholder,  
onBlur,  
placeholder,  
disabled,  
deactivateFilter = false,  
className,  
iconType = ICON_TYPES.ARROW_DOWN_FIT,  
isReadOnly,  
errorMessage,  
label,  
hasLabelIcon,  
hasLabelInInput,  
subText,  
mode = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
isExposed = false,  
isExposedSmall = false,  
dropdownMenuButton,  
shouldScrollIntoView = true,  
isFixedDropdown = false,  
...rest
}: IComboboxProps): JSX.Element => {  
const [itemsFilter, setItemsFilter] = useState('');  
const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
if (!a.order || !b.order) {      
return 0;    
const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => {    
if (!a.order || !b.order) {      
return 0;    
}    
return a.order - b.order;  
});
  const inputItems = itemsFilter    
? sortedOptions.filter((option) =>        
new RegExp(`\\b${escapeNewRegExpString(itemsFilter)}`, 'i').test(option.label ?? '')      
)    
: sortedOptions;
  const stateReducer = (_state: UseComboboxState<IOption>, actionAndChanges: any): any => {    
const { type, changes } = actionAndChanges;    
const { selectedItem, inputValue } = changes;
    switch (type) {      
case useCombobox.stateChangeTypes.InputClick:        
return {          
...changes,          
isOpen: _state.isOpen, // do not toggle the menu when input is clicked.        
};      
case useCombobox.stateChangeTypes.InputChange:        
if (selectedItem && inputValue === '') {          
return {            
...changes,            
return {            
...changes,            
selectedItem: null,          
};        
}        
return changes;      
default:        
return changes;    
}  
};
  const handleChange = useCallback(    
({ selectedItem }: { selectedItem?: any }) => {      
setItemsFilter('');      
if (selectedItem?.value || value) {        
onChange(name, selectedItem?.value || '');      
}    
},    
[name, onChange, value]  
);
  const handleBlur = (e: React.FocusEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLButtonElement | HTMLDivElement>): void => {    
// Using a timeout, because the blur event will fire way faster than onInputValueChange    
// and we will get a validation error for a split second, for a valid value.    
// Also, tabing from the input to the button will fire the validation.    
// Firing onBlur only if the next active element, doesn't contain a class that starts with Combobox    
// opened an issue in `downshift` and will follow it for a better approach:    
// https://github.com/downshift-js/downshift/issues/1095#issuecomment-672323045    
const { tagName } = e.target;    
if (tagName === 'BUTTON' || tagName === 'INPUT') {      
requestAnimationFrame(() => {        
if (!document.activeElement?.className.includes('Combobox')) {          
const selectedItem = inputItems.find((item) => item.label?.toLowerCase() === itemsFilter.toLocaleLowerCase());
          if (selectedItem) {            
handleChange({ selectedItem });          
} else if (value) {            
handleChange({              
selectedItem: sortedOptions.find((item) => item.value === value),            
});          
}
          onBlur?.();        
}      
});    
}  
};
  const handleHighlightChange = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>, id: string): void => {    
const target = e.currentTarget;    
const menu = document.getElementById(`${id}-menu`);    
const menuBottomPosition = menu?.getBoundingClientRect().bottom || 0;    
if (menuBottomPosition >= window.innerHeight && shouldScrollIntoView) {      
const menu = document.getElementById(`${id}-menu`);    
const menuBottomPosition = menu?.getBoundingClientRect().bottom || 0;    
if (menuBottomPosition >= window.innerHeight && shouldScrollIntoView) {      
target.scrollIntoView();    
}  
};  
const {    
getInputProps,    
getItemProps,    
getMenuProps,    
getToggleButtonProps,    
highlightedIndex,    
isOpen,    
openMenu,    
selectedItem,    
setHighlightedIndex,  
} = useCombobox({    
id,    
selectedItem: options.find((option) => option.value === value) || null,    
items: inputItems,    
stateReducer,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
});
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
});
  const labelRef = useRef<HTMLLabelElement | null>(null);  
const comboboxRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);  
const menuRef = useRef<HTMLUListElement | null>(null);
  /**   
* This is here to clear the filtered items when a user searches for a selection   
* but the selects the same item that was previously selected   
*/  
useEffect(() => {    
if (!isOpen) {      
setItemsFilter('');    
}  
}, [isOpen]);
  const { 'aria-labelledby': ariaLabelledBy, ...restWithoutAriaLabelledBy } = rest ?? {};
  const getToggleAriaLabel = (): string => {    
if (label) {      
return `Toggle ${label.toLowerCase()} dropdown menu`;    
}    
if (placeholder) {      
return `Toggle ${placeholder.toLowerCase().replace('select ', '')} dropdown menu`;    
}    
return 'Toggle dropdown menu';  
};
}    
return 'Toggle dropdown menu';  
};
  return (    
<SCCombobox      
ref={comboboxRef}      
className={`Combobox ${isOpen ? 'isOpen' : ''}`}      
onBlur={handleBlur}      
mode={mode}      
tabIndex={-1} // Traps the focus inside the Combobox, when switching from input to button      
onClick={() => {        
/**         
* Brief timeout used to give SCMenu ref an opportunity to open         
* and have its bottom position be correctly evaluated.         
*/        
setTimeout(() => {          
const menuBottom =            
menuRef.current && menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()              
? menuRef.current.getBoundingClientRect().bottom              
: 0;          
// Scroll into view only if necessary to show options out of view.          
if (menuBottom >= window.innerHeight && comboboxRef.current && shouldScrollIntoView) {            
comboboxRef.current?.scrollIntoView();          
}        
}, 10);      
}}>      
{label && (        
<SCComboboxLabel ref={labelRef} mode={mode} disabled={disabled} hasLabelInInput={hasLabelInInput} htmlFor={id}>          
{hasLabelInInput ? `${label}: ` : label}{' '}          
{hasLabelIcon && <SCComboboxLabelIcon name={ICON_TYPES.INFORMATION} size={16} />}        
</SCComboboxLabel>      
)}      
<SCInputWrap className='Combobox__input-wrap' isExposed={isExposed}>        
<SCComboboxInput          
{...getInputProps({            
id,            
name,            
onFocus: (e) => {              
if (!e.target.value) {                
if (!isOpen) {                  
openMenu();                
}              
} else {                
e.target.select();              
}            
},            
onKeyDown: (e) => {              
if (e.key === UP_ARROW_KEY || e.key === DOWN_ARROW_KEY) {                
handleHighlightChange(e, id);              
}            
},            
onKeyUp: (e) => {              
const inputElement = e.target as HTMLInputElement; // Must cast due to incorrect type coming from the lib.              
if (shouldFilterOnKeyUp(e.key) && inputElement.value !== selectedItem?.label) {                
setItemsFilter(inputElement.value ?? '');              
}            
},            
disabled: disabled || deactivateFilter,            
placeholder,            
...rest,            
mode,          
})}          
isExposed={isExposed}          
isExposedSmall={isExposedSmall}          
errorMessage={errorMessage}          
mode={mode}          
readOnly={isReadOnly || isExposed}          
hasLabelInInput={hasLabelInInput}          
labelWidth={labelRef.current?.offsetWidth}          
className={`Combobox__input ${className}`}        
/>        
<SCMenuToggle          
{...getToggleButtonProps({            
'className': 'Combobox__menu-toggle',            
'tabIndex': 0,            
'id': `${id}-toggle`,            
'aria-expanded': isOpen,            
'aria-haspopup': 'listbox',            
'onClick': () => {              
requestAnimationFrame(() => {                
setHighlightedIndex(-1);              
});            
},            
disabled,            
...restWithoutAriaLabelledBy,          
})}          
className={className}          
errorMessage={errorMessage}          
isExposed={isExposed}          
isExposedSmall={isExposedSmall}          
hasLabelInInput={hasLabelInInput}          
mode={mode}          
aria-label={getToggleAriaLabel()}>          
<Icon name={iconType} size={14} />        
</SCMenuToggle>      
</SCInputWrap>      
{errorMessage && !hasLabelInInput && (        
<SCInputErrorMessage id={`${id}-error`} data-testid={`${id}-error`}>          
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.ALERT} size={16} /> {errorMessage}        
</SCInputErrorMessage>      
)}      
{subText && <SCComboboxSubtext mode={mode}>{subText}</SCComboboxSubtext>}      
<FixedContainer fixed={isFixedDropdown} deps={[isOpen]}>        
<SCMenuWrap isExposed={isExposed} isOpen={isOpen || !!className?.includes('__selected')}>          
<SCMenu            
mode={mode}            
{...getMenuProps({              
className: `Combobox__menu ${className}`,              
ref: menuRef,            
})}>            
{(isOpen || className?.includes('__selected')) && (              
<>                
{inputItems.length > 0 ? (                  
inputItems.map((item, index) => (                    
<SCMenuItem                      
key={item.value}                      
mode={mode}                      
{...getItemProps({                        
item,                        
index,                        
'role': 'option',                        
'aria-selected': selectedItem === item || item.value === 'storybook-active',                        
'id': `${id}-item-${index}`,                        
'className': `Combobox__menu-item ${highlightedIndex === index ? 'highlighted' : ''} ${                          
selectedItem === item || item.value === 'storybook-active' ? 'selected' : ''                        
} ${item.disabled ? 'disabled' : ''}`,                      
})}>                      
{item.label}                      
{(selectedItem === item || item.value === 'storybook-active') && (                        
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} />                      
)}                    
</SCMenuItem>                  
))                
) : (                  
<SCNoOptionsMessage>No options.</SCNoOptionsMessage>                
)}                
{dropdownMenuButton && (                  
<CoreButton                    
id='combobox-menu-button'                    
size='small'                    
displayMode={DisplayMode[mode]}                    
onClick={dropdownMenuButton.onClick}>                    
{dropdownMenuButton.label}                  
</CoreButton>                
onClick={dropdownMenuButton.onClick}>                    
{dropdownMenuButton.label}                  
</CoreButton>                
)}              
</>            
)}          
</SCMenu>        
</SCMenuWrap>      
</FixedContainer>    
</SCCombobox>  
);
};
Combobox.displayName = 'Combobox';
export { Combobox };

```typescript
import { ICON_TYPES, Icon } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import React, { useCallback } from 'react';
import { SCDiv, SCIconContainer, SCButton } from './styles';
import { IContainerWithIconProps } from './types';
/** 
* Container component that accepts a child element and contains a positionable `Icon` button. 
*/
const ContainerWithIcon = ({ children, name, action, position, ...props }: IContainerWithIconProps): JSX.Element => {  
const handleClick = useCallback(() => action(), [action]);  
return (    
<SCDiv>      
{/* conditionally render children to enforce natural readable tab order */}      
{name !== ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_UP && children}      
<SCIconContainer position={position} size={16}>        
<SCButton onClick={handleClick} {...props}>          
<Icon name={name} size={16} />        
</SCButton>      
</SCIconContainer>      
{name === ICON_TYPES.ARROW_TRIANGLE_UP && children}    
</SCDiv>  
);
};
export { ContainerWithIcon };
```

import { ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
export enum Position {  
TOP_RIGHT = 'TOP_RIGHT',  
BOTTOM_RIGHT = 'BOTTOM_RIGHT',  
MIDDLE_RIGHT = 'MIDDLE_RIGHT',
}
export interface IAriaProps {  
/**   
* Extra WAI-ARIA text to indicate the state of the collapsible element (optional).   
*/  
ariaExpanded?: boolean;  
/**   
* Extra WAI-ARIA text for screen readers (optional).   
*/  
ariaLabel?: string;
}
export interface IContainerWithIconProps extends IAriaProps {  
/**   
* Elements to be displayed within the container.   
*/  
children: JSX.Element | JSX.Element[] | string;  
/**   
* The type of the icon -- makes use of the `ICON_TYPE` enum.   
*/  
/**   
* Extra WAI-ARIA text for screen readers (optional).   
*/  
ariaLabel?: string;
}
export interface IContainerWithIconProps extends IAriaProps {  
/**   
* Elements to be displayed within the container.   
*/  
children: JSX.Element | JSX.Element[] | string;  
/**   
* The type of the icon -- makes use of the `ICON_TYPE` enum.   
*/  
name: ICON_TYPES;  
/**   
* The position of the icon -- makes use of the `Position` enum.   
*/  
position: Position;  
/**   
* Optional click handler, triggered when the icon is pressed.   
*/  
action: () => void;

import React from 'react';
import { useSelect } from 'downshift';
import { ISelectDropdownOption } from 'core/interfaces';
import { SCSROnlyLabel } from 'styles/shared';
import { ICON_TYPES, Icon } from '../Icon';
import { IContextMenuProps } from './types';
import { SCContextMenu, SCTrigger, SCMenu, SCMenuItems, SCMenuItem, SCPaddedIcon, SCMeatball } from './styles';
/** 
* @component ContextMenu 
* Provides the Context Menu component from Core UI Kit. 
* 
* @param {string} id? 
* @param {string} className? 
* @param {boolean} alwaysOpen? - Force menu to always be open 
* @param {string} ariaLabel - Used to ensure a11y when label is not provided 
* @param {boolean} disabled? - When disabled, context menu cannot be opened 
* @param {(value: string) => void} onClick -  What to do when the selected value is changed 
* @param {ISelectDropdownOption[]} options? - List of dropdown options to show in menu 
* @param {string} value? - Currently selected index value of the options array 
*/
const ContextMenu = ({  
id,  
className,  
alwaysOpen,  
ariaLabel,  
disabled,  
onClick,  
options,  
value = '',
}: IContextMenuProps): JSX.Element => {  
const selectedItem = options.find((option) => option.value === value) ?? null;
  const handleChange = ({ selectedItem }: { selectedItem?: ISelectDropdownOption | null }): void => {    
const selectValue = selectedItem?.value ?? '';    
onClick(selectValue);  
};
  const { isOpen, getLabelProps, getToggleButtonProps, getMenuProps, highlightedIndex, getItemProps } = useSelect({    
id,    
items: options,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
});  
const menuProps = getMenuProps({    
className: 'ContextMenu__menu',  
});
  const getClassNameForMenuItem = (item: ISelectDropdownOption, index: number): string => {    
let result = 'ContextMenu__menu-item';    
});
  const getClassNameForMenuItem = (item: ISelectDropdownOption, index: number): string => {    
let result = 'ContextMenu__menu-item';    
if (highlightedIndex === index) {      
result += ' highlighted';    
}    
if (selectedItem === item) {      
result += ' selected';    
}    
return result;  
};
  return (    
<>      
<SCSROnlyLabel {...getLabelProps()}>{ariaLabel}</SCSROnlyLabel>      
<SCContextMenu id={id} className={`ContextMenu ${className} ${isOpen || alwaysOpen ? 'isOpen' : ''}`}>        
<SCTrigger {...getToggleButtonProps({ className: 'ContextMenu', disabled })} aria-controls={menuProps.id}>          
<SCMeatball name={ICON_TYPES.MEATBALLS_MENU} size={20} />        
</SCTrigger>        
<SCMenu isOpen={isOpen || alwaysOpen}>          
<SCMenuItems {...menuProps}>            
{(isOpen || alwaysOpen) && (              
<>                
{options.map((item, index) => (                  
<SCMenuItem                    
<SCMenuItem                    
{...getItemProps({                      
key: item.value,                      
item,                      
index,                      
className: getClassNameForMenuItem(item, index),                    
})}>                    
{item.jsxLabel ? item.jsxLabel : item.label}                    
{selectedItem === item && (                      
<SCPaddedIcon>                        
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} />                      
</SCPaddedIcon>                    
)}                  
</SCMenuItem>                
))}              
</>            
)}          
</SCMenuItems>        
</SCMenu>      
</SCContextMenu>    
</>  
);
};
export { ContextMenu };
item,                      
index,                      
className: getClassNameForMenuItem(item, index),                    
})}>                    
{item.jsxLabel ? item.jsxLabel : item.label}                    
{selectedItem === item && (                      
<SCPaddedIcon>                        
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} />                      
</SCPaddedIcon>                    
)}                  
</SCMenuItem>                
))}              
</>            
)}          
</SCMenuItems>        
</SCMenu>      
</SCContextMenu>    
</>  
);
};

export const CoreButton = ({

children,  
className,  
onClick,  
readOnly,  
disabled,  
tabIndex,  
size = '',  
shape = Shape.Rounded,  
displayMode = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
isSecondary = false,  
type = 'button',  
noWrap,  
loading,  
isGhost,  
...props
}: IProps) => {  
return (    
<SCButton      
id={id}      
data-testid={id}      
ref={forwardedRef}      
className={`${className || ''} ${readOnly ? 'readonly' : ''}`}      
onClick={onClick}      
type={type}      
disabled={disabled || readOnly || loading}      
shape={shape}      
displayMode={displayMode}      
tabIndex={tabIndex}      
size={ButtonSizes[`${size}` as ButtonSizes]}      
noWrap={noWrap}      
isSecondary={isSecondary}      
isGhost={isGhost}      
{...props}>      
{(!loading || isGhost) && children}      
{loading && !isGhost && (        
<SCButtonContentContainer>          
{children}          
<SCButtonContentContainerInner data-testid={`core-button-spinner-${id}`} displayMode={displayMode}>            
<Spinner width={25} height={25} />          
</SCButtonContentContainerInner>        
</SCButtonContentContainer>      
)}    
</SCButton>  
);
};
/** 
* ForwardRefCoreButton 
</SCButtonContentContainer>      
)}    
</SCButton>  
);
};
/** 
* ForwardRefCoreButton

*/
export const ForwardRefCoreButton = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, IProps>((props, ref) => (  
<CoreButton forwardedRef={ref} {...props} />
));

}  
return css`

return secondaryDisplayModeStyles;  
}  
if (displayMode === DisplayMode[displayMode]) {    
return primaryDisplayModeStyles;  
}  
return undefined;

import React, { useContext, useRef } from 'react';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { SelectAllMode } from 'core';
import { ColorModeContext } from 'styles/modes';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import { a11yKeydown } from 'components/atoms/a11y';
import {  
SCCheckBoxContainer,  
SCA11yCheckBox,  
SCCheckBox,  
SCCheckIcon,  
SCChildren,  
SCErrorDisplay,  
SCPaddedIcon,  
SCErrorLabel,
} from './styles';
import { ICoreCheckBoxProps, Size } from './types';
/** 
* @component CoreCheckBox 
* @requires ColorModeContext 
* Consists of a visible checkbox and uses a hidden checkbox for accessibility. 
* 
* @todo when old CheckBox component is fully deprecated, rename this component 
* 
* @param {string} id? 
* @param {string} className? 
* @param {string} ariaLabel [REQUIRED] 
* @param {string | JSX.Element} children? 
* @param {Size} size? - small (default) or large checkbox 
* @param {Size} size? - small (default) or large checkbox 
* @param {boolean} checked? - Whether the checkbox is activated to show the check icon 
* @param {boolean} disabled? - Disables onChange and affects styles 
* @param {(value: boolean) => void} onChange? 
* @param {boolean} isParentClickable? - Whether or not clicking outside the checkbox counts 
* @param selectAllDisplay - Whether or not to display the select all checkbox and label 
* @param selectAllPartial - Select All partial selection state 
* @param selectAllSelected - Select All total all selected selection state 
* @param {string} errorText? - Error condition text to show under CheckBox 
* @param props 
*/
const CoreCheckBox = ({  
id,  
className,  
ariaLabel,  
children,  
forwardedRef,  
tabIndex,  
size = Size.SMALL,  
checked = true,  
disabled = false,  
onChange = (): void => {},  
onBlur,  
isParentClickable = true,  
isParentClickable = true,  
selectAllDisplay,  
selectAllPartial = false,  
selectAllSelected = false,  
errorText = '',  
...props
}: ICoreCheckBoxProps): JSX.Element => {  
const colorMode = useContext(ColorModeContext);  
const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const getIconName = ({ colorMode, size }: { colorMode: DisplayMode; size: Size }): ICON_TYPES => {    
if (selectAllDisplay === SelectAllMode.ENABLED && selectAllPartial && !selectAllSelected) {      
if (size === Size.LARGE) {        
return colorMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ICON_TYPES.MINUS_SMALL_WHITE : ICON_TYPES.MINUS_SMALL_BLACK;      
}      
return colorMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ICON_TYPES.MINUS_SMALL_WHITE : ICON_TYPES.MINUS_SMALL_BLACK;    
}    
if (size === Size.LARGE) {      
return colorMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_WHITE : ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK;    
}    
return colorMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT ? ICON_TYPES.SMALL_CHECKMARK_WHITE : ICON_TYPES.SMALL_CHECKMARK_BLACK;  
};  
const testIdLabel = selectAllDisplay === SelectAllMode.ENABLED ? 'checkbox-select-all' : 'checkbox';  
const titleId = id ? `${id}-title` : 'checkbox-title';  
const ariaProps = ariaLabel ? { 'aria-label': ariaLabel } : { 'aria-labelledby': titleId };

<>

disabled={disabled}

size={size}        
mode={colorMode}        
isParentClickable={isParentClickable}        
onClick={(): void => {

onMouseDown={(e) => {

}        
}}        
{...props}>        
<SCA11yCheckBox          
tabIndex={tabIndex}

styleSize={size}          
mode={colorMode}          
onChange={(): void => {}}          
disabled={disabled}          
onKeyDown={(e: KeyboardEvent | React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>): void => {            
if (a11yKeydown(e)) {              
e.preventDefault();              
if (!disabled) {                
onChange(!checked);              
}            
}          
}}          
onClick={(): void => {            
if (!disabled && !isParentClickable) {              
onChange(!checked);            
}          
}}          
onBlur={() => {            
onBlur?.(checked);          
}}        
/>        
<SCCheckBox          
data-testid={testIdLabel}          
checked={!!(selectAllPartial || selectAllSelected || checked)}          
size={size}          
mode={colorMode}          
errorText={errorText}          
disabled={disabled}>          
<SCCheckIcon            
name={getIconName({ colorMode, size })}            
checked={!!(selectAllPartial || selectAllSelected || checked)}            
size={size === Size.LARGE ? 24 : 12}          
/>        
</SCCheckBox>        
<SCChildren disabled={disabled} size={size} mode={colorMode} id={titleId}>          
{children}        
</SCChildren>      
</SCPaddedIcon>          
<SCErrorLabel>{errorText}</SCErrorLabel>        
</SCErrorDisplay>      
)}    
</>  
);
};

return Theme.getColor('error');

background: ${({ mode, disabled, checked }): IPropsHook | string => {

if (checked && mode === DisplayMode.LIGHT) {

import React from 'react';
import { Direction } from 'core/enums';
import {  
SCDottedProgressBar,  
SCDot,  
SCLine,  
SCLegendContainer,  
SCLegendText,  
SCLegendLine,

SCLegendConnectorLine,
} from './styles';
import { IDottedProgressBarProps } from './types';
/** 
* DottedProgressBar 
* @summary 
* Generic dotted progress bar 
* Default color is brand T1, length of progress bar will expand to fit container 
* @param displayMode 
* Optional displayMode prop to override default brand T1 color 
* DisplayMode.DARK = contrastWhite, DisplayMode.LIGHT = contrastBlack 
* @param totalDots 
* Total dots (steps) in the progress bar 
* @param filledDots 
* Number of dots filled in progress bar (steps completed) 
* @param dotDiameter 
* Width and height of each dot (pixels) 
* @param legend 
* Optional array of strings to be used as labels above dots. 
* Number of strings (legend.length) should equal totalDots number 
*/
const DottedProgressBar = ({  
totalDots,  
filledDots,  
totalDots,  
filledDots,  
dotDiameter = 8,  
legend = [],  
displayMode,
}: IDottedProgressBarProps): JSX.Element => {  
const dotData = Array.from({ length: totalDots }).map((_, i) => ({    
filled: i + 1 <= filledDots,  
}));
  const hasLegend = legend.length > 0;
  return (    
<SCDottedProgressBar hasLegend={hasLegend}>      
{dotData.map((data, dotIndex) => {        
const key = `line-segment-${dotIndex}`;        
const dotProps = {          
filled: data.filled,          
dotDiameter,          
displayMode,        
};        
const legendText = legend[dotIndex] || '';        
return hasLegend ? (          
<React.Fragment key={key}>            
<SCLegendContainer>              
<SCLegendText displayMode={displayMode} aria-label={legendText}>                
{legendText}              
</SCLegendText>              
<SCDot id={`dot-${dotIndex}`} {...dotProps} />              
{dotIndex !== 0 && (                
<SCLegendConnectorLine dotDiameter={dotDiameter} displayMode={displayMode} direction={Direction.LEFT} />              
)}              
{dotIndex !== dotData.length - 1 && (                
<SCLegendConnectorLine                  
dotDiameter={dotDiameter}                  
displayMode={displayMode}                  
direction={Direction.RIGHT}                
/>              
)}            
</SCLegendContainer>            
{dotIndex !== legend.length - 1 && <SCLegendLine dotDiameter={dotDiameter} displayMode={displayMode} />}          
</React.Fragment>        
) : (          
<React.Fragment key={key}>            
<SCDot {...dotProps} />            
{dotIndex < dotData.length - 1 && <SCLine totalDots={totalDots} displayMode={displayMode} />}          
</React.Fragment>        
);      
})}    
</SCDottedProgressBar>  
{dotIndex < dotData.length - 1 && <SCLine totalDots={totalDots} displayMode={displayMode} />}          
</React.Fragment>        
);      
})}    
</SCDottedProgressBar>  
);
};
export { DottedProgressBar };
```

import styled, { CSSProp, css } from 'styled-components';
import { Direction } from 'core/enums';
import { Theme } from 'styles';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
const getProgressBarColor = (displayMode?: DisplayMode): CSSProp => {  
if (displayMode === DisplayMode.DARK) {    
return css`      
${Theme.getColor('contrastWhite')}    
`;  
}  
if (displayMode === DisplayMode.LIGHT) {    
return css`      
${Theme.getColor('contrastBlack')}    
`;  
}  
return css`    
${Theme.getColor('T1')}  
`;
};
export const SCDottedProgressBar = styled.div<{ hasLegend: boolean }>`  
display: flex;  
export const SCDottedProgressBar = styled.div<{ hasLegend: boolean }>`  
display: flex;  
flex-direction: row;  
align-items: ${({ hasLegend }): string => (hasLegend ? 'flex-end' : 'center')};
`;
export const SCDot = styled.div<{  
filled?: boolean;  
dotDiameter: number;  
displayMode?: DisplayMode;
}>`  
${({ dotDiameter }): string => `    
height: ${dotDiameter}px;    
width: ${dotDiameter}px;    
border-radius: ${dotDiameter}px;  
`}  
${({ filled, displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
filled      
? css`          
background-color: ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};        
`      
: css`          
border: 1px solid ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};        
`}
`;
`}
`;
export const SCLine = styled.div<{  
totalDots: number;  
displayMode?: DisplayMode;
}>`  
height: 1px;  
${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
css`      
background-color: ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};    
`}  
${({ totalDots }): string => `width: calc(100% / ${totalDots - 1 || 1});`}
`;
export const SCLegendContainer = styled.div`  
display: flex;  
flex-direction: column;  
align-items: center;  
justify-content: flex-end;  
position: relative;
`;
export const SCLegendText = styled.div<{ displayMode?: DisplayMode }>`  
margin-bottom: 8px;  
${Theme.getFontStyles('body3')};  
${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
${Theme.getFontStyles('body3')};  
${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
css`      
color: ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};    
`}
`;
export const SCLegendLine = styled.div<{  
dotDiameter: number;  
displayMode?: DisplayMode;
}>`  
${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
css`      
background-color: ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};    
`}  
height: 1px;  
width: 100%;  
margin-bottom: ${({ dotDiameter }): string => `${dotDiameter / 2}px`};
`;
export const SCLegendConnectorLine = styled.div<{  
dotDiameter: number;  
direction: Direction.LEFT | Direction.RIGHT;  
displayMode?: DisplayMode;
}>`  
position: absolute;  
position: absolute;  
bottom: ${({ dotDiameter }): string => `${dotDiameter / 2}px`};  
${({ direction, dotDiameter }): string =>    
direction === Direction.RIGHT      
? `left: calc(50% + ${dotDiameter / 2}px)`      
: `right: calc(50% + ${dotDiameter / 2}px)`};  
width: ${({ dotDiameter }): string => `calc(50% - ${dotDiameter / 2}px)`};  
height: 1px;  
${({ displayMode }): CSSProp =>    
css`      
background-color: ${getProgressBarColor(displayMode)};    
`}
`;

import React from 'react';
import { Direction } from 'core/enums';
import { SCDottedProgressButtons, SCDot, SCButton } from './styles';
import { IDottedProgressButtonsProps } from './types';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from '../Icon';
/** 
* DottedProgressButtons 
* @summary 
* Generic dotted progress buttons. Dots indicate the progress and two buttons provide control. 
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from '../Icon';
/** 
* DottedProgressButtons 
* @summary 
* Generic dotted progress buttons. Dots indicate the progress and two buttons provide control. 
* @param totalDots 
* Total dots (steps) in the progress bar 
* @param activeDot 
* Dot that is filled, indexed from 0. *NOTE:* Will be ignored if `filledDots` is provided. 
* @param dotDiameter 
* Width and height of each dot (pixels) 
* @param onClickButtons 
* A click handler for when dots are clicked. 
* @param onClickDots 
* A click handler for when the left and right buttons are clicked. 
* @param aria: { dotLabel: string; leftLabel: string; rightLabel: string; activeDotLabel?: string; } 
* Various `aria-label`s for sub-components. 
* @param: id?: string; 
* Html attribute id for this element. 
*/
const DottedProgressButtons = ({  
totalDots: length,  
activeDot = 0,  
onClickButtons,  
onClickButtons,  
onClickDots,  
id,  
aria: { dotLabel, leftLabel, rightLabel, activeDotLabel },
}: IDottedProgressButtonsProps): JSX.Element => {  
const isOnFirst = activeDot === 0;  
const isOnLast = activeDot >= length - 1;
  return (    
<SCDottedProgressButtons id={id}>      
<li aria-current={false}>        
<SCButton          
id={`${id}-left`}          
type='button'          
disabled={isOnFirst}          
aria-label={leftLabel}          
onClick={() => {            
onClickButtons(Direction.LEFT);          
}}>          
<Icon name={isOnFirst ? ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_LEFT_GRAY : ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_LEFT} />        
</SCButton>      
</li>      
{Array.from({ length }).map((_, dotIndex) => (        
<li aria-current={dotIndex === activeDot}>          
<SCDot            
type='button'            
id={`${id}-dot-${dotIndex}`}            
// eslint-disable-next-line react/no-array-index-key            
key={`dot-${dotIndex}`}            
onClick={() => onClickDots(dotIndex)}            
selected={dotIndex === activeDot}            
aria-label={dotIndex === activeDot && activeDotLabel ? activeDotLabel : `${dotLabel} ${dotIndex + 1}`}          
/>        
</li>      
))}      
<li aria-current='false'>        
<SCButton          
id={`${id}-right`}          
type='button'          
disabled={isOnLast}          
onClick={() => {            
onClickButtons(Direction.RIGHT);          
}}          
aria-label={rightLabel}>          
<Icon name={isOnLast ? ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_RIGHT_GRAY : ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_RIGHT} />        
</SCButton>      
</li>    
</SCDottedProgressButtons>  
);
<Icon name={isOnLast ? ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_RIGHT_GRAY : ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_RIGHT} />        
</SCButton>      
</li>    
</SCDottedProgressButtons>  
);
};
export { DottedProgressButtons };
```

import React from 'react';
import { useSelect } from 'downshift';
import { ICON_TYPES, Icon } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import { ISelectDropdownOption } from 'core';
import { SCSROnlyLabel } from 'styles/shared';
import { IDropdownProps } from './types';
import {  
SCStylizedDropdown,  
SCLabel,  
SCLabelWrap,  
SCPaddedIcon,  
SCTrigger,  
SCTriggerIcon,  
SCMenuWrapper,  
SCTrigger,  
SCTriggerIcon,  
SCMenuWrapper,  
SCMenu,  
SCMenuItems,  
SCMenuItem,  
SCOptionButton,  
SCBeforeOptionWrap,
} from './styles';
const OPTION_BUTTON_VALUE = 'OPTION_BUTTON_VALUE';
const defaultTriggerIcon = <Icon name={ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON} size={16} />;
const defaultDisabledTriggerIcon = <Icon name={ICON_TYPES.CHEVRON_DISABLED} size={16} />;
/** 
* @deprecated - Use the Combobox component with the isExposed prop 
* @component Dropdown 
* @param {string} id? 
* @param {string} className? 
* @param {JSX.Element | string} label? - The text above the clickable trigger element (AKA the "eyebrow") 
* @param {JSX.Element} labelIcon? - Optional icon to go next to the label 
* @param {string} ariaLabel? - Used to ensure a11y when label is not provided 
* @param {JSX.Element | string} triggerElement (REQUIRED) - Clickable element to open options menu 
* @param {JSX.Element | null} triggerIcon? - Optional icon to go next to triggerElement 
* @param {JSX.Element | null} triggerIconDisabled? - Icon to show when dropdown is disabled 
* @param {string} hideTriggerIcon? - Optional flag to hide triggerIcon 
* @param {ISelectDropdownOption[]} options? - List of dropdown options to show in menu 
* @param {string} name? - Optional name identifier that is passed to the onChange 
* @param {string} value? - Currently selected index value of the options array 
* @param {(name: string, value: string) => void} onChange? -  What to do when the selected value is changed 
* @param {string} optionText: (REQUIRED) Text for button at the bottom of the options menu 
* @param {() => void} onOptionButtonSelect? 
* @param {boolean} disabled? - When disable Dropdown menu cannot be opened 
* @param {boolean} alwaysOpen? - Force Dropdown menu to always be open 
* @param {JSX.Element | string} beforeOptionElement? - Optional element to display inside the option menu, before the options 
*/
const Dropdown = ({  
id,  
className = '',  
label,  
labelIcon,  
ariaLabel,  
triggerElement,  
triggerIcon = defaultTriggerIcon,  
triggerIconDisabled = defaultDisabledTriggerIcon,  
hideTriggerIcon = false,  
options = [],  
name = '',  
value = '',  
onChange,  
optionText,  
onOptionButtonSelect,  
disabled,  
alwaysOpen,  
beforeOptionElement,  
alignDropdownLeft,
}: IDropdownProps): JSX.Element => {  
const handleChange = ({ selectedItem }: { selectedItem?: ISelectDropdownOption | null }): void => {    
const selectValue = selectedItem?.value ?? '';    
if (selectValue === OPTION_BUTTON_VALUE) {      
onOptionButtonSelect && onOptionButtonSelect();    
} else if (selectValue !== value && onChange) {      
onChange(name, selectValue);      
selectedItem?.action && selectedItem.action();    
}  
};
  const sortedOptions = options.sort((a, b) => (!a.order || !b.order ? 0 : a.order - b.order));
  const buttonOption: ISelectDropdownOption = { label: optionText || '', value: OPTION_BUTTON_VALUE };  
const sortedOptionsWithButton = optionText ? sortedOptions.concat(buttonOption) : sortedOptions;
  const { isOpen, getLabelProps, getToggleButtonProps, getMenuProps, highlightedIndex, getItemProps } = useSelect({    
id,    
items: sortedOptionsWithButton,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
  const { isOpen, getLabelProps, getToggleButtonProps, getMenuProps, highlightedIndex, getItemProps } = useSelect({    
id,    
items: sortedOptionsWithButton,    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: handleChange,  
});
  const selectedItem = options.find((option) => option.value === value) ?? null;
  const getClassNameForMenuItem = (item: ISelectDropdownOption, index: number): string => {    
let result = 'StylizedDropdown__menu-item';    
if (highlightedIndex === index) {      
result += ' highlighted';    
}    
if (selectedItem === item) {      
result += ' selected';    
}    
return result;  
};
  const menuProps = getMenuProps({    
className: 'StylizedDropdown__menu',  
});
  // If neither label nor ariaLabel is provided, fall back on the trigger element text.  
const ariaOnlyLabel = ariaLabel || (typeof triggerElement === 'string' ? triggerElement : '');
  // When disabled and triggerIconDisabled is provided, use that, otherwise use triggerIcon  
const triggerIconElement = (disabled && triggerIconDisabled) || triggerIcon;
});
  // If neither label nor ariaLabel is provided, fall back on the trigger element text.  
const ariaOnlyLabel = ariaLabel || (typeof triggerElement === 'string' ? triggerElement : '');
  // When disabled and triggerIconDisabled is provided, use that, otherwise use triggerIcon  
const triggerIconElement = (disabled && triggerIconDisabled) || triggerIcon;
  return (    
<>      
{label && (        
<SCLabelWrap>          
<SCLabel {...getLabelProps()} className='StylizedDropdown__label'>            
{label}          
</SCLabel>          
{labelIcon && <SCPaddedIcon>{labelIcon}</SCPaddedIcon>}        
</SCLabelWrap>      
)}      
{!label && <SCSROnlyLabel {...getLabelProps()}>{ariaOnlyLabel}</SCSROnlyLabel>}
      <SCStylizedDropdown id={id} className={`StylizedDropdown ${className} ${isOpen || alwaysOpen ? 'isOpen' : ''}`}>        
<SCTrigger          
{...getToggleButtonProps({ className: 'StylizedDropdown__trigger', disabled })}          
aria-controls={menuProps.id}>          
{triggerElement}          
{!hideTriggerIcon && !!triggerIconElement && <SCTriggerIcon>{triggerIconElement}</SCTriggerIcon>}        
</SCTrigger>        
<SCMenuWrapper>          
<SCMenu {...menuProps} alignDropdownLeft={alignDropdownLeft}>            
{(isOpen || alwaysOpen) && (              
<>                
{beforeOptionElement && <SCBeforeOptionWrap>{beforeOptionElement}</SCBeforeOptionWrap>}                
<SCMenuItems>                  
{sortedOptions.map((item, index) => (                    
<SCMenuItem                      
{...getItemProps({                        
key: item.value,                        
item,                        
index,                        
className: getClassNameForMenuItem(item, index),                      
})}>                      
{item.jsxLabel ? item.jsxLabel : item.label}                      
{selectedItem === item && (                        
<SCPaddedIcon>                          
<Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={16} />                        
</SCPaddedIcon>                      
)}                    
</SCMenuItem>                  
))}                
</SCMenuItems>                
{optionText && (                  
<SCOptionButton                    
size='small'                    
{...getItemProps({                      
key: buttonOption.value,                      
item: buttonOption,                      
index: sortedOptions.length,                    
})}>                    
{optionText}                  
</SCOptionButton>                
)}              
</>            
)}          
</SCMenu>        
</SCMenuWrapper>      
</SCStylizedDropdown>    
</>  
);
};
export { Dropdown };

import { useSelect } from 'downshift';
import React from 'react';
import { IOption, ISelectDropdownOption } from 'core';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { Icon, ICON_TYPES } from 'components/atoms/Icon';
import {  
IconWrapper,  
SCDropdownUtility,  
SCLabel,  
SCMenu,  
SCMenuItem,  
SCMenuToggle,  
SCNoOptionsMessage,  
SCNoOptionsMessage,  
SCToggleWrapper,
} from './styles';
const DropdownUtility = ({  
id,  
value = '',  
className,  
leftIcon,  
options,  
onChange,  
disabled,  
iconType = ICON_TYPES.ARROW_DOWN_FIT,
}: {  
id: string;  
value: string;  
className?: string;  
leftIcon?: React.ReactNode;  
options: IOption[];  
onChange: (option: IOption) => void;  
disabled?: boolean;  
iconType?: ICON_TYPES;
}): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();
  const {    
  const {    
getItemProps,    
getMenuProps,    
getToggleButtonProps,    
highlightedIndex,    
isOpen,    
selectedItem,    
setHighlightedIndex,  
} = useSelect({    
id,    
items: options,    
selectedItem: options.find((x) => x.value === value),    
itemToString: (item) => item?.label || '',    
onSelectedItemChange: ({ selectedItem }: { selectedItem?: ISelectDropdownOption | null }): void => {      
const newValue = selectedItem?.value ?? '';      
if (newValue !== value && onChange) {        
onChange({          
label: selectedItem?.label ?? '',          
value: newValue,        
});        
selectedItem?.action && selectedItem.action();      
}    
},  
});
},  
});
  const selectedLabel = selectedItem?.label ?? value;
  return (    
<SCDropdownUtility      
className={`DropdownUtility ${className} ${isOpen ? 'isOpen' : ''}`}      
aria-label={t('MS_COMBOBOX__MENU_TOGGLE_ARIA')}>      
<SCToggleWrapper        
{...getToggleButtonProps({          
className: 'DropdownUtility__toggle-wrapper',          
tabIndex: 0,          
onClick: () => {            
requestAnimationFrame(() => {              
setHighlightedIndex(-1);            
});          
},          
disabled,        
})}>        
{!!leftIcon && <IconWrapper>{leftIcon}</IconWrapper>}        
<SCLabel className='DropdownUtility__toggle-wrapper-label'>{selectedLabel}</SCLabel>        
<SCMenuToggle className='DropdownUtility__toggle-wrapper-menu-toggle'>          
<Icon name={iconType} size={14} />        
</SCMenuToggle>      
</SCToggleWrapper>      
<SCMenu {...getMenuProps({ className: 'DropdownUtility__menu' })}>        
{isOpen &&          
options.length > 0 &&          
options.map((item, index) => {            
const highlightedClass = highlightedIndex === index ? 'highlighted' : '';            
const selectedClass = selectedItem?.value === item?.value ? 'selected' : '';            
return (              
<SCMenuItem                
key={item.value}                
{...getItemProps({                  
item,                  
index,                  
className: `DropdownUtility__menu-item ${highlightedClass} ${selectedClass}`,                
})}>                
{item.label}                
{selectedItem === item && <Icon name={ICON_TYPES.LARGE_CHECKMARK_BLACK} size={14} />}              
</SCMenuItem>            
);          
})}        
{isOpen && options.length === 0 && <SCNoOptionsMessage>{t('MS_COMBOBOX__NO_RESULTS')}</SCNoOptionsMessage>}      
</SCMenu>    
</SCDropdownUtility>  
);
};
};
DropdownUtility.displayName = 'DropdownUtility';
export { DropdownUtility };
```

import React from 'react';
import { a11yKeydown } from 'components/atoms/a11y';
import { IEditButtonProps } from './types';
import { SCButton, SCDisableButton, SCButtonContentWrapper } from './styles';
const EditButton = ({ id, ariaLabel, disabled = false, handleNavigation }: IEditButtonProps): JSX.Element => {  
const VIEW_POSITION = 'center';
  const handleFocus = (e: React.FocusEvent<HTMLButtonElement>): void => {    
e.target.scrollIntoView({      
block: VIEW_POSITION,      
inline: VIEW_POSITION,    
});  
};
  const onKeyDownNavigationHandler = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>): void => {    
if (a11yKeydown(e)) {      
handleNavigation();    
}  
  const onKeyDownNavigationHandler = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>): void => {    
if (a11yKeydown(e)) {      
handleNavigation();    
}  
};
  return (    
<SCDisableButton disabled={disabled}>      
<SCButton        
id={id}        
aria-label={ariaLabel}        
onKeyDown={onKeyDownNavigationHandler}        
onMouseDown={handleNavigation}        
onFocus={handleFocus}>        
<svg viewBox='0 0 40 24' xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg'>

import React from 'react';

import { SCFlexError } from './styles';

/** 
* Displays text for error messages with optional icon. 
* @param error 
* @param showIcon 
import { IErrorMessageProps } from './types';
/** 
* Displays text for error messages with optional icon. 
* @param error 
* @param showIcon 
* @param id optional id used in automated testing 
*/
const ErrorMessage = ({  
error = '',  
showIcon,  
id,  
colorMode,  
className,  
ariaLabel,  
ariaLabeledby,
}: IErrorMessageProps): JSX.Element => {  
const icon = <Icon size={16} name={ICON_TYPES.ERROR} ariaLabel='Error' focusable />;  
const flexErrorProps = {    
...(className && { className }),    
...(id && { id }),    
...(colorMode && { colorMode }),  
};  
return (    
<SCFlexError {...flexErrorProps}>      
<div>        
{showIcon && icon}{' '}        
<span role='status' aria-label={ariaLabel} aria-labelledby={ariaLabeledby}>          
{error}        
</span>      
</div>    
</SCFlexError>  
);
};
export { ErrorMessage };

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { SCContainerDiv } from './styles';
import { IFixedContainerProps } from './types';
/** 
* A container that will position its children as fixed in the same spot. 
*/
const FixedContainer = ({ fixed = false, className = '', children }: IFixedContainerProps): JSX.Element => {  
const markerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);  
const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);  
/**   
* This function runs continuously while fixed is true,   
* so it directly updates the DOM and avoids React refreshes for better performance.   
* if className is defined then neight top or width is set it is up to the caller to override   
*/  
const refresh = () => {    
if (!fixed) {      
return;    
}    
const rect = markerRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();    
const container = containerRef.current;    
if (rect && container) {      
const { top, width } = rect;

const CheckBoxGroup: FunctionComponent<ICheckBoxGroupProps> = ({

onChange?.(checkValues);

? setSelectAllPartial(true)

import * as React from 'react';
import { Size } from 'core/enums';
import { DisplayMode } from 'styles/themes';
import { SCRadioButtonContainer, HiddenRadioButton, SCRadioButton, ChildContainer } from './styles';
import { IRadioButtonProps } from './types';
const doNothing = (): void => {};
/** 
* Basic radio input component. 
* Consists of a visible radio and uses a hidden input for accessibility. 
*/
// TODO: Remove this component and replace it with RadioInput. This component has multiple accessibility issues:
// the label should be the text associated with the radio button, right now it's used just for styling without content,
// should have onChange event instead of the onClick event on the div wrapper (has no keyboard functionality as a side effect), etc
const RadioButton = ({  
name,  
id,  
children,  
value,  
checked = false,  
disabled = false,  
colorScheme = DisplayMode.LIGHT,  
size = Size.DEFAULT,  
className,  
ariaLabel = '',  
isItActionButton = false,  
onChange = (): void => {},  
error = false,  
hasLabelDescription,
}: IRadioButtonProps): JSX.Element => (  
<SCRadioButtonContainer    
className={`${className} ${disabled && 'disabled'}`}    
disabled={disabled}    
checked={checked}    
size={size}    
colorScheme={colorScheme}    
onClick={(): false | void => !disabled && onChange(value)}    
hasLabelDescription={hasLabelDescription}    
error={error}>    
<HiddenRadioButton      
name={name}      
aria-label={ariaLabel}      
checked={checked}      
onChange={doNothing}      
disabled={disabled}      
id={id ?? value}    
/>    
<SCRadioButton      
htmlFor={id ?? value}      
checked={checked}      
isItActionButton={isItActionButton}      
colorScheme={colorScheme}      
disabled={disabled}      
size={size}      
error={error}    
/>    
<ChildContainer size={size}>{children}</ChildContainer>  
</SCRadioButtonContainer>
);
export { RadioButton };

radioSize = Size.DEFAULT,

onChange = (): void => {},

error = false,  
disabled = false,  
className,  
radioGroupVariant = undefined,

}: IRadioGroupProps): JSX.Element => {

<SCRadioGroup      
role='radiogroup'

error={error}      
variant={radioGroupVariant}      
{...rest}>      
{options.map((element, i) => (        
<SCRadioOption          
name={name}          
id={`${name}-option-${i + 1}-${element.value}`}          
alignment={alignment}          
checked={value === element.value}          
value={element.value}          
key={element.value}          
size={radioSize}          
colorScheme={displayMode}          
onChange={onChange}          
ariaLabel={getAriaLabel(element)}          
showVerticalDivider={showVerticalDivider}

{element.labelDescription ? (            
<SCRadioChildren>              
{element.label}

{element.labelDescription}              
</SCLabelDescription>            
<SCRadioChildren>              
{element.label}

{element.labelDescription}              
</SCLabelDescription>            
</SCRadioChildren>          
) : (

)}        
</SCRadioOption>      
))}    
</SCRadioGroup>

```typescript
export interface IRangeStepperInputProps {  
id?: string;  
/**   
* Value of the RangeStepperInput.   
*/  
value?: number;  
/**   
* Array of number value options displayed in the RangeStepperInput.   
*/  
range?: number[];  
/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon RangeStepperInput value change.   
*/  
onChange?: (name: string, value: string) => void;  
/**   
* Optional callback function, triggered upon RangeStepperInput blur.   
*/  
onBlur?: (name: string, value: string) => void;  
/**   
* Optional prefix preceding the value in the RangeStepperInput.   
*/  
prefix?: JSX.Element | string;  
/**   
* Boolean indicating if there's an error with the RangeStepperInput value.   
*/  
hasError?: boolean;  
/**   
* Name attribute to get the input.   
*/  
name: string;  
/**   
* Boolean indicating if the RangeStepperInput is read-only.   
*/  
readOnly?: boolean;  
/**   
* Extra WAI-ARIA text for screen readers (optional).   
*/  
labelledBy: string;
labelledBy,  
onChange = (): void => {},  
onBlur = (): void => {},  
placeholder,  
...props
}: IStepperInputProps): JSX.Element => {  
const getValueInBoundaries = useCallback((value: number): number => Math.max(Math.min(value, max), min), [min, max]);
  const [isFocused, setFocused] = useState(false);  
const exitFocusMode = useCallback((): void => {    
setFocused(false);  
}, [setFocused]);  
const enterFocusMode = useCallback((): void => {    
setFocused(true);  
}, [setFocused]);
  const getOutValue = useCallback(    
(stringValue: string): number | undefined => {      
const value = formatter ? formatter.out(stringValue) : parseInt(stringValue, 10);
      if (!value && value !== 0) return undefined;
      return getValueInBoundaries(value);    
},    
[getValueInBoundaries, formatter]  
);
  const onChangeHandler = useCallback(    
(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => onChange(getOutValue(e.target.value)),    
      if (!value && value !== 0) return undefined;
      return getValueInBoundaries(value);    
},    
[getValueInBoundaries, formatter]  
);
  const onChangeHandler = useCallback(    
(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => onChange(getOutValue(e.target.value)),    
[onChange, getOutValue]  
);
  const onBlurHandler = useCallback(    
(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {      
onBlur(getOutValue(e.target.value));      
exitFocusMode();    
},    
[onBlur, getOutValue, exitFocusMode]  
);
  const getInputValue = useCallback((): string => {    
if (!value && value !== 0) return '';    
return value.toString();  
}, [value]);
  const handleButtonClick = useCallback(    
(gain: number): void => {      
if (props.readOnly) return;      
const newValue = getValueInBoundaries((value || 0) + gain);      
(gain: number): void => {      
if (props.readOnly) return;      
const newValue = getValueInBoundaries((value || 0) + gain);      
onChange(newValue);      
onBlur(newValue);    
},    
[value, props.readOnly, onChange, onBlur, getValueInBoundaries]  
);
  const onPlusClickHandler = useCallback(() => handleButtonClick(interval), [interval, handleButtonClick]);
  const onMinusClickHandler = useCallback(() => handleButtonClick(-interval), [interval, handleButtonClick]);
  return (    
<StepperInputView      
{...props}      
id={id}      
labelledBy={labelledBy}      
onChange={onChangeHandler}      
onFocus={enterFocusMode}      
onBlur={onBlurHandler}      
onPlusClick={onPlusClickHandler}      
onMinusClick={onMinusClickHandler}      
value={!isFocused && formatter ? formatter.in(value) : getInputValue()}      
placeholder={placeholder}    
/>  
);
onMinusClick={onMinusClickHandler}      
value={!isFocused && formatter ? formatter.in(value) : getInputValue()}      
placeholder={placeholder}    
/>  
);
};
export { StepperInput };

import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { useTypedTranslation } from 'i18n';
import { ColorModeContext } from 'styles/modes';
import { SCStepperInput, SCButtons, SCWrapper } from './styles';
import { IStepperInputViewProps } from './types';
/** 
* @requires ColorModeContext 
* Contains text input and increase/decrease stepper buttons. 
* Accepts handlers from `StepperInput` component. 
*/
const StepperInputView = ({  
id,  
value,  
prefix = '',  
onChange = (): void => {},  
onFocus = (): void => {},  
onBlur = (): void => {},  
onPlusClick = (): void => {},  
onMinusClick = (): void => {},  
readOnly,  
name,  
labelledBy,  
labelledBy,  
hasError,  
className,  
placeholder,
}: IStepperInputViewProps): JSX.Element => {  
const { t } = useTypedTranslation();  
const mode = useContext(ColorModeContext);
  return (    
<SCWrapper className={className}>      
<SCStepperInput hasError={hasError} mode={mode} readOnly={readOnly}>        
<span aria-hidden='true'>{prefix}</span>        
<input          
id={`${id}_input`}          
type='text'          
aria-labelledby={labelledBy}          
aria-live='polite'          
tabIndex={0}          
name={name}          
value={value}          
onChange={onChange}          
onFocus={onFocus}          
onBlur={onBlur}          
readOnly={readOnly}          
placeholder={placeholder}        
/>      
</SCStepperInput>      
<SCButtons>        
<button          
id={`${id}_button-plus`}          
type='button'          
aria-describedby={labelledBy}          
aria-label={t('STEPPER_INPUT_VIEW__ARIA_INCREASE')}          
onClick={onPlusClick}>          
<p>+</p>        
</button>        
<button          
id={`${id}_button-minus`}          
type='button'          
aria-describedby={labelledBy}          
aria-label={t('STEPPER_INPUT_VIEW__ARIA_DECREASE')}          
onClick={onMinusClick}>          
<p>-</p>        
</button>      
</SCButtons>    
</SCWrapper>  
);
};
export { StepperInputView };
</button>      
</SCButtons>    
</SCWrapper>  
);
};
export { StepperInputView };
```

import React, { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { StyledComponent } from 'styled-components';
import { SCUtilityInput } from 'styles/utility';
import { ITextAreaProps } from './types';
import { ITextAreaProps } from './types';
import { SCTextAreaWrapper } from './styles';
/** 
* 
* @summary 
* Basic Textarea component 
* It display depend on display mode prop and also changing theme depend on state of input. 
*/
const TextArea = ({  
id,  
name,  
onChange,  
onBlur,  
disabled,  
placeholder,  
className,  
value,  
label,  
displayMode,  
isDynamic,
}: ITextAreaProps): JSX.Element => {  
const ref = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {    
if (ref.current && isDynamic) {      
if (ref.current && isDynamic) {      
ref.current.style.height = 'auto';      
const scrollheight = ref.current.scrollHeight;      
ref.current.style.height = `${scrollheight}px`;    
}  
}, [value]);  
// Typescript has trouble understanding the "as" prop in styled components.  
const TextArea = SCUtilityInput as StyledComponent<'textarea', any, {}, never>;  
return (    
<SCTextAreaWrapper colorScheme={displayMode} isDynamic={isDynamic}>      
<TextArea        
ref={ref}        
as='textarea'        
id={id}        
name={name}        
onChange={onChange}        
onBlur={onBlur}        
className={className}        
disabled={disabled}        
placeholder={placeholder}        
aria-invalid        
value={value}        
aria-label={label}        
rows={isDynamic ? 1 : 2}      
/>    
</SCTextAreaWrapper>  
);
};
export { TextArea };
```

* Boolean denoting for whether the textarea field is disabled.   
* Set to resize: none, text content expands textarea   
*/  
isDynamic?: boolean;
}
export interface ITextAreaValidProps {  
invalid: boolean;
}
export interface ITextAreaColorProps {  
colorScheme: string | undefined;
}

```typescript
import React, { useRef, useEffect, SyntheticEvent } from 'react';
import closeIcon from '../static/close.svg';
import { LOXButton as Button } from '../LOXButton';
import { IGrowl } from './types';
import {  
SCGrowlWrapper,  
SCGrowlIconWrapper,  
SCGrowlTextContainer,  
SCGrowlTitle,  
SCGrowlMessage,  
SCGrowlButtonWrapper,
} from './styles';
SCGrowlMessage,  
SCGrowlButtonWrapper,
} from './styles';
export interface Event extends SyntheticEvent {}
const Messages = {  
GenericTitle: 'Uh-oh! It looks like something went wrong.',  
GenericMessage: 'This could be an error message.',
};
export const Growl = ({  
className = '',  
buttonClassName = '',  
title,  
message,  
buttonId,  
icon,  
iconComponent,  
variant = 'Default',  
onClose,  
timeout,  
showCloseButton = true,  
children,  
...restProps
}: IGrowl) => {  
const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
children,  
...restProps
}: IGrowl) => {  
const buttonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {    
if (showCloseButton && timeout && buttonRef.current) {      
const timer = setTimeout(() => {        
buttonRef.current?.click();      
}, timeout);
      return () => {        
clearTimeout(timer);      
};    
}
    return () => {};  
}, [showCloseButton, timeout, buttonRef.current]);
  return (    
<SCGrowlWrapper variant={variant} className={className} {...restProps}>      
{icon && (        
<SCGrowlIconWrapper>          
<img src={icon} alt='' />        
</SCGrowlIconWrapper>      
)}      
{iconComponent && <SCGrowlIconWrapper>{iconComponent}</SCGrowlIconWrapper>}      
<SCGrowlTextContainer>        
<SCGrowlTitle>{variant === 'Generic Error' ? Messages.GenericTitle : title}</SCGrowlTitle>        
<SCGrowlMessage>{variant === 'Generic Error' ? Messages.GenericMessage : message}</SCGrowlMessage>        
{children}      
</SCGrowlTextContainer>      
{showCloseButton && (        
<SCGrowlButtonWrapper>          
<Button            
buttonStyle='Accessibility'            
className={buttonClassName}            
id={buttonId}            
data-testid={buttonId}            
onClick={onClose}            
ref={buttonRef}>            
<img alt='close growl' src={closeIcon} />          
</Button>        
</SCGrowlButtonWrapper>      
)}    
</SCGrowlWrapper>  
);
};
```
<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_END -->

</SCGrowlButtonWrapper>      
)}    
</SCGrowlWrapper>  
);
};

import styled, { SimpleInterpolation } from 'styled-components';
export const SCGrowlWrapper = styled.div<{ variant: string }>`  
display: flex;  
align-items: center;  
border-radius: 20px;  
padding: 16px;  
margin: 8px auto;  
box-sizing: border-box;  
box-shadow: 0px 4px 8px rgb(0 0 0 / 6%);  
${(props): SimpleInterpolation => {    
if (props.variant === 'Default') {      
return `        
background: #f1f7ff;        
border: 1px solid #5597fb;      
`;    
}    
if (props.variant === 'Warning') {      
return `        
background: #fff7ef;        
border: 1px solid #fbaf5f;        
white-space: pre-line;      
`;    
}    
if (props.variant === 'Success') {      
return `        
background: #eafdf7;        
border: 1px solid #269b78;      
`;    
}
`;    
}
    return `      
background: #faf5f8;      
border: 1px solid #cd2155;    
`;  
}}
`;
export const SCGrowlIconWrapper = styled.div`  
min-width: 24px;  
min-height: 24px;  
height: 100%;  
display: flex;  
margin: 5px 24px auto 0;
`;
export const SCGrowlTextContainer = styled.div`  
font-size: 14px;  
font-weight: normal;
`;
export const SCGrowlTitle = styled.div`  
font-weight: 500;  
line-height: 20px;  
color: #262c30;  
word-break: break-word;
`;    
}    
if (props.variant === 'Success') {      
return `        
background: #eafdf7;        
border: 1px solid #269b78;      
`;    
}
    return `      
background: #faf5f8;      
border: 1px solid #cd2155;    
`;  
}}
`;
export const SCGrowlIconWrapper = styled.div`  
min-width: 24px;  
min-height: 24px;  
height: 100%;  
display: flex;  
margin: 5px 24px auto 0;
`;
export const SCGrowlTextContainer = styled.div`  
font-size: 14px;  
font-weight: normal;
}
`;

import { HtmlHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
/*
Currently supported variants:
- Default (blue)
- Error (red)
- Warning (yellow)
- Success (green)
*/
export interface IGrowl extends HtmlHTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> {  
title?: string;  
message?: string;  
buttonId: string;  
onClose: React.MouseEventHandler<HTMLButtonElement>;  
timeout?: number;  
icon?: string;  
iconComponent?: React.ReactElement;  
variant?: 'Default' | 'Error' | 'Generic Error' | 'Warning' | 'Success';  
className?: string;  
buttonClassName?: string;  
showCloseButton?: boolean;  
id?: string;  
children?: JSX.Element;
}
```
